### [152] [Shrome at Touché: Soft-Vote Ensembling and Counter-Causal Augmentation for Causality Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03268)
*Roham Zendehdel Nobari,Shayan Sooratgar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究新闻文本中的反因果识别，构建检测、因果跨度抽取和极性分类三个模型；在CCNC测试集上取得检测F1 0.869、极性宏F1 0.817，抽取得分0.728且为参赛系统最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规因果抽取系统容易依赖“导致”“引起”等表面词语，因此会将“人们误以为X导致Y”这类否认因果关系的表达误判为正向因果。研究旨在将反因果识别纳入新闻因果抽取，并应对反因果标注样本稀缺的问题。

Method: 针对因果检测、原因与结果跨度抽取、极性分类分别训练模型。检测器采用微调分类器，并利用抽取出的跨度实施跨任务规则以减少误报；抽取器集成三个RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF标注器，通过平均token级分数后解码；极性分类中，使用基于九种反因果表达模式提示大语言模型生成的句子扩充训练数据，并通过自动结构检查筛选。

Result: CCNC留出测试集上，因果检测F1为0.869，极性分类宏F1为0.817。组织方最终仅针对因果样本评估跨度抽取，系统取得粒度调整F1 0.728，为所有提交系统（含组织方基线）中的最高分。开发集仅用于组件选择和消融实验。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合跨任务规则、token级分数集成和经结构筛选的合成数据，有助于处理反因果新闻表达；该系统在官方抽取评估中表现领先。摘要未提供显著性检验或跨数据集验证，因此结论主要适用于CCNC及其评测设置。

Abstract: Touché 2026 extends causality extraction to counter-causal claims: news sentences whose surface form appears causal but whose meaning denies the causation, as in "It is falsely believed that X caused Y." A system that relies on surface cues such as "caused" or "led to" will accept such a sentence as causal and give it the wrong polarity. On the Countercausal News Corpus (CCNC), the task has three subtasks: deciding whether a sentence is causal (detection), locating its cause and effect spans (extraction), and labeling its polarity as procausal, counter-causal, or uncausal. We build one model per subtask. Detection is a fine-tuned classifier with a single cross-task rule that uses the extracted spans to remove false positives. For extraction, we ensemble three RoBERTa-large BILOU+CRF taggers by averaging their token-level scores before decoding, rather than voting on the spans each tagger produces. For polarity, where labeled counter-causal examples are scarcest, we add training sentences generated by a large language model prompted with nine patterns of counter-causal expression adapted from Hagen et al., keeping only those that pass automatic structural checks. On the held-out CCNC test set, the system reaches F1 0.869 on detection and macro-F1 0.817 on polarity, and in the organizers' final causal-only evaluation of extraction it scores granularity-adjusted F1 0.728, the highest extraction score among all submissions including the organizers' baseline. The development split is used only for component selection and the ablations reported in the paper.

</details>


### [153] [To Jev or Not? Evaluating the Accuracy and Efficiency of Structured Decision Models for Hate-Speech Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03324)
*Demetris Paschalides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 HATEDECIDE，比较六种结构化决策模型配置在四个仇恨言论数据集上的分类表现，并评估定义提示、问题拆解、延迟与成本。实验显示，商用 LLM 的优势有限且不稳定；较优托管决策模型在诊断集上以约低 97% 的推理成本，达到距最佳商用 LLM 仅 1.6 个宏平均 F1 分数的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线内容规模扩大，加上 LLM 使有害内容更易生成和改写，令仇恨言论审核面临效率与适应不同定义的双重挑战。研究关注结构化决策模型能否在无需针对任务训练的情况下，利用自然语言标准进行可靠、低成本的审核。

Method: 在四个仇恨言论数据集上评估六种结构化决策模型配置，并与专用审核模型、零样本方法、商用模型及监督式基线比较。研究还测试提供数据集定义、将定义拆解为多个问题对分类效果的影响，并测量推理延迟和成本；另在诊断测试集上比较模型表现。

Result: 商用 LLM 仅在一个数据集上显著优于所有决策模型。提供定义会改变最多 28% 的预测，但分类效果并未稳定提升；问题拆解仅在 20% 的比较中显著改善表现。在诊断集上，表现最佳的托管决策模型与最佳商用 LLM 的宏平均 F1 差距为 1.6 分，推理成本约低 97%。

Conclusion: 结构化决策模型可能为审核提供成本更低的选择，但其表现取决于数据集与设置。显式标准和增加问题步骤并不能可靠提升分类效果，因此部署时应基于具体数据集进行评测，并同时权衡准确性、延迟与成本。

Abstract: The scale of online content makes hate-speech moderation challenging, while Large Language Models (LLMs) enable harmful material to be produced and adapted more easily. Moderation therefore requires efficient classifiers that can accommodate different definitions of hate speech. Recent structured decision models accept natural-language criteria and select among specified answers, raising the question of whether they can meet these requirements without task-specific training. We present HATEDECIDE, an evaluation of six decision-model configurations on four hate-speech datasets against specialized moderation, zero-shot, commercial, and supervised baselines. We examine whether supplying a dataset's definition, or decomposing it into multiple questions, improves classification, and we measure their latency and cost. We find that commercial LLMs significantly outperform all decision models on only one dataset. Supplying definitions changes up to 28\% of predictions without consistently improving classification, and decomposition significantly improves performance in only 20\% of the comparisons. On a diagnostic set of test cases, the best hosted decision model comes within 1.6 macro-F1 points of the best commercial LLM at approximately 97\% lower inference cost. These results identify opportunities for inexpensive moderation, while showing that explicit criteria and additional questions do not reliably improve classification.

</details>


### [154] [SyntaxBench: A Statistical Diagnostic Framework for Character-Level Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03329)
*Mohsen Larni,Sobhan Ebrahimi Azar,Pouyan Nahed,Kazem Taghva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 SyntaxBench，用于系统评估大语言模型的字符级推理能力，并发现分词方式、推理模式会影响表现，而较难的子串提取任务仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字符级推理评估多依赖孤立测试和总体准确率，难以区分输入形式、分词机制与推理模式的影响，也不足以刻画模型在具体任务上的错误。

Method: 构建 SyntaxBench，涵盖字符计数、字母包含、回文检测、编辑距离、最长字符串选择五项核心任务，以及更具挑战性的 index_to_span 子串提取任务。核心任务配对使用英文与字符长度匹配的随机字符串；六项任务均设置零样本、一样本和四样本提示。评测 8 个、规模为 2B–32B 的开放权重模型及 11 种推理模式配置，并采用精确匹配与宽松准确率、Cohen’s kappa、配对 McNemar 检验、bootstrap 置信区间、Kendall’s tau、分类别指标、分词分析及多重比较校正等方法。

Result: 随机字符串比英文具有更高的字符可见度（每 token 1.892 个字符，对比 3.169），英文单词占用的 token 越多，字符计数准确率越低。推理模式的作用因模型和任务而异：Gemma4-31B 在接近饱和的任务上变化很小；Qwen3.6-27B 在四样本回文检测中开启思考后准确率由 0.952 降至 0.886。index_to_span 仍表现困难，最佳四样本精确匹配准确率仅为 6.75%。

Conclusion: 字符级推理评估不应只报告总体准确率；需要控制输入条件，进行配对统计检验，并分析分词与推理模式的影响。基准结果也显示，长文本中的子串提取仍是模型的明显短板。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used where small syntactic errors matter, yet character-level reasoning is still evaluated mostly through isolated probes and aggregate accuracy. We introduce SyntaxBench, a diagnostic benchmark and statistical evaluation framework for character-level reasoning. It contains five core tasks, character counting, letter containment, palindrome detection, edit distance, and longest-string selection, plus index_to_span, a harder substring-extraction stress test. The five core tasks use paired English and character-length-matched random-string inputs. index_to_span documents share a 200-500 word band and are not character-length matched. All six tasks use zero-, one-, and four-shot prompts.
  We evaluate eight open-weight models from 2B to 32B parameters across 11 reasoning-mode configurations. The framework reports exact-match and relaxed accuracy, Cohen's kappa, paired McNemar tests with odds ratios, bootstrap confidence intervals, Kendall's tau, class-conditional metrics, tokenization analysis, and multiple-comparison-corrected tests. Three findings stand out. First, tokenization shapes accuracy: random strings are more character-visible than English strings (1.892 vs. 3.169 characters per token), and character-counting accuracy falls as English words occupy more tokens. Second, reasoning mode is not uniformly helpful: Gemma4-31B is nearly unchanged across modes on the near-saturated tasks, while Qwen3.6-27B is worse with thinking on palindrome detection (0.952 non-thinking vs. 0.886 thinking at four-shot). Third, index_to_span remains largely unsolved; the best four-shot exact-match accuracy is 6.75%.
  Character-level evaluation needs controlled inputs, paired tests, and analyses of tokenization and reasoning mode rather than aggregate accuracy alone.

</details>

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, but knowledge-intensive visual question answering often requires external textual evidence beyond the image and the model's parametric knowledge. Existing multimodal RAG systems commonly rely on Top-$K$ retrieval or reranking, which may return redundant passages and provide limited control over whether an answer update is sufficiently supported by the retrieved evidence. We propose \textit{CLIMB}, a training-free inference-time framework for multimodal RAG. CLIMB first constructs a compact complementary evidence pool using an MMR-style objective that balances query relevance and passage-level redundancy. It then performs confidence-controlled refinement within this fixed pool: an R/E/C critic scores passages by relevance, evidence specificity, and cross-modal alignment, while an evidence-grounded confidence estimator accepts an updated answer only when the estimated confidence increases. This design provides a simple stopping criterion and reduces unnecessary refinement without modifying the underlying retriever or MLLM. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that CLIMB consistently improves over retrieval-augmented multimodal baselines. Ablations further indicate that complementary pooling, critic-based scoring, and iterative confidence-controlled refinement each contribute to the final performance.

</details>

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses reference solutions as privileged hindsight to supervise student-generated reasoning trajectories. However, reference-based guidance may explain a correct solution without addressing why the student's own reasoning fails. This reasoning mismatch between the guidance provided and the correction needed can encourage the student to borrow correct conclusions while leaving its reasoning errors unresolved. Moreover, applying the same hindsight throughout the trajectory risks a distillation trap, where unnecessary constraints on valid reasoning compete with correction of substantive errors. To address these issues, we propose Root-Cause-Guided On-Policy Distillation (RC-OPD), which uses repairs of the student's own reasoning to provide guidance that addresses its specific errors while building on valid progress. For each failed attempt, RC-OPD locates the earliest substantive error, develops a local correction, and uses the corrected intermediate result as an anchor for the valid prefix. An iterative diagnosis--repair--continuation process tests the repairs through student continuation, identifying further errors within a fixed repair budget. For repair chains that reach a correct answer, root--cause--guided distillation uses failure diagnoses and corrective goals to supervise the erroneous segments, while anchor-guided distillation supports the corresponding valid prefixes with reasoning chains leading to the repaired intermediate results. We evaluate RC-OPD across multiple datasets and model scales. Extensive experiments and analyses show that it mitigates reasoning mismatch and the distillation trap, yielding substantial performance gains.

</details>

Abstract: Table-to-report generation refers to the task of automatically generating article-level analyt- ical reports from relational tables and is an essential capability for automated data science and decision support. Its central challenge lies in systematically discovering verifiable com- posite insights across tables, attributes, and analytical perspectives, and organizing them into coherent, complete, and traceable evidence chains. Existing methods primarily rely on sequential, reactive data agents or direct Large Language Model(LLM) generation. They suffer from exploration bias: early local observations constrain subsequent actions, causing models to focus prematurely on local analyzes and miss cross-table or cross-dimensional evidence. We propose ComInsight, which reformulates insight discovery as the composition of atomic evidences. We first define an atomic insight as the smallest executable analytical unit conforming to a predefined analysis pattern and enumerate all valid atomic insights from database schema and content. These atoms are then organized into a multi-relational insight graph, where nodes represent verified data facts and edges encode logical, temporal, or hierarchical relations. Finally, a set of composition operators systematically fuses atomic nodes into higher-order composite conclusions. Every composite output is accompanied by executable SQL and fine-grained provenance, ensuring full verifiability. Across three benchmarks InsightBench, DDR-Bench, and T2R-Bench, ComInsight consistently outperforms strong baselines in factual correctness, novelty, and structural completeness. We believe ComInsight offers a reliable, efficient, and explainable path toward table-to-report generation.

</details>

Abstract: Authorship Attribution (AA) requires capturing fine-grained stylistic characteristics, making it particularly challenging in zero-shot (ZS) settings where no task-specific supervision is available. In this work, we investigate the effect of author representations on ZS AA by evaluating a label-only prompting baseline together with three author representation strategies: representative writing samples, LLM-generated descriptions, and style embeddings (LISA). The first three approaches perform attribution using LLM prompting, while the embedding-based approach uses style embeddings with cosine similarity. We investigate the influence of prompt design and propose a two-stage embedding-based attribution framework that combines candidate space reduction with embedding-dimension selection. The results show that label-only ZS AA is ineffective, while incorporating author-specific representations consistently improves attribution performance. Among the evaluated approaches, the proposed two-stage LISA framework achieves the strongest overall performance, whereas LLM-generated style descriptions provide a substantially more compact representation of author style at the cost of some attribution performance. These findings demonstrate the importance of author representation in ZS AA, while indicating that current open-source LLMs remain insufficient for robust attribution without more effective representation learning.

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic systems for three PAN at CLEF 2026 shared tasks (Reasoning Trajectory Detection, Voight-Kampff Generative AI Detection, and Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis), unified by a shared analytical framework: feature robustness under distribution shift is governed by support overlap between training and test distributions, not by training-set effect size. This yields a taxonomy (domain-anchored, domain-portable, domain-invariant) that explains why generator-specific features die under domain shift while vocabulary fingerprints (hapax ratio, Yule's K, Heaps' exponent), compression measures, and character n-grams survive. On Reasoning Trajectory Detection, where training was entirely mathematics and 84 percent of test was unseen domains, the framework guided system design to 1st place in source detection (0.85 macro F1 via Opus-Sonnet agreement) and 3rd place in safety classification (0.66 macro F1 via query-refusal decomposition). For Voight-Kampff, we built a calibrated ensemble of DeBERTa-v2 (ONNX), multi-seed LightGBM with 44 domain-portable stylometric features, and SVM on n-gram TF-IDF, combined via learned stacking with isotonic calibration; the best configuration achieved 0.891 on the PAN 2026 test set with balanced sub-metrics (0.853 to 0.902 across all evaluation dimensions). For Multi-Author Writing Style Analysis, we describe a system fusing spectral clustering over character n-gram similarity graphs, normalized compression distance for local boundary detection, and SmolLM-135M perplexity for neural change-point detection; a platform mix-up meant our run never reached the official evaluation, so we report the design and its a priori predictions. Across all three tasks, features measuring generation process properties are designed to outperform features measuring generated content properties under domain shift.

</details>

Abstract: We describe the Writerslogic team's participation in the CLEF 2026 SimpleText shared task, addressing Task 1 (text simplification) and Task 2 (complexity spotting). For Task 1, we develop a multi-candidate generation pipeline using GPT-4o-mini that produces five simplification candidates per sentence at varying temperatures, then selects the best candidate using a reference-free scoring heuristic that rewards compression, source word retention, Cochrane Plain Language Summary vocabulary usage, and lexical simplicity. On Task 1.1 (sentence-level simplification), our Claude Sonnet 4 submission achieves SARI 47.43 and BLEU 14.21, the top-ranked sentence-level system (3rd on the combined Task 1 leaderboard, behind two document-level submissions). For Task 2, we fine-tune a DeBERTa-v3-large NLI model on 350K labeled (source, sentence) pairs, framing hallucination detection as natural language inference. The model reads the most relevant source sentence as premise and the candidate as hypothesis, directly learning to distinguish grounded from hallucinated content. On Task 2.1 (binary overgeneration identification), our fine-tuned DeBERTa system achieves 0.8081 document-level macro F1 (0.8085 in our best ensemble), the top-ranked entry within the identification track and 2nd among teams overall, behind AIIR Lab (0.8197). On Task 2.2 (multi-class error classification), our best submission reaches 0.804 multiclass accuracy, ranking 2nd among unique teams behind AIIR Lab (0.827). We evaluate both tasks on English and multilingual biomedical text from Cochrane systematic reviews.

</details>

Abstract: Relational databases are among the most widely deployed forms of structured knowledge, and natural language access to them requires grounding language onto schema entities and relations while handling the ambiguity inherent in how people phrase requests. Existing synthetic NL-to-SQL data generation methods largely ignore this ambiguity and produce oversimplified queries that fail to prepare models for the complexity of real-world structured knowledge access. We present FALCON, a framework that generates realistic, ambiguity-aware NL-to-SQL data matching the complexity of challenging real-world benchmarks, at low cost using compact open models. Our approach combines reserved-word SQL seeding and persona-based prompting to generate structurally complex queries, while alignment-based filtering preserves difficulty by distinguishing genuinely incorrect examples from complex but valid queries. Human evaluation confirms consistent high quality across model sizes, and our generated data exceeds existing benchmarks in both SQL complexity and natural language richness. Difficulty-stratified analysis shows models trained on FALCON data increasingly outperform baseline-trained models as query complexity increases, validating our pipeline's success in generating challenging training data. When combined with a small proportion of existing benchmark data, mixed training recovers performance on simpler queries while preserving these advantages on complex ones. The model- and database-agnostic design enables organizations to generate high-complexity NL-to-SQL training data locally without external APIs.

</details>

Abstract: Modern chess engines are silent experts: they play at a superhuman level, but do not offer explanations for their play. On the other hand, language models (LMs) can generate plausible-sounding explanations, but their weak playing strength limits the utility of their explanations. We introduce Queen, a 4B-parameter chess-language model that can explain its moves and plans while playing at the level of a typical Grandmaster. Our novel framework enables domain-specific reasoning through complementary components: an encoder-decoder architecture and an iterative distillation algorithm. This architecture integrates a silent expert chess encoder with an instruction-tuned LM through cross-attention, which we train via a question-answering curriculum to extract chess concepts from the encoder's representations. Building on this domain-adapted model, we iteratively improve its explanations with a natural-language analog of the Bellman update: the model analyzes the positions after its top candidate moves and consolidates them into an explanation of the current position, which is then distilled back into the model. Over seven iterations, our model gains over 900 Elo points (1782 to 2697), substantially surpassing all frontier models on both playing strength and puzzle accuracy, despite containing three orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Furthermore, LM-based evaluations show that our explanations are fluent and approach GPT-5.6-Sol (high) in coherence. The generality of our architecture and training procedure suggests a recipe for applying language models to domains where silent expert encoders are available, like games, robotics, and computer use.

</details>

### [164] [MintFlow: Minimal Trajectory Intervention for Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02260)
*Yesom Park,Kelvin Kan,Qifan Chen,Thomas Flynn,Hayden Schaeffer. Xihaier Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MintFlow 是一种无需训练的受约束流匹配采样框架，通过对中间流状态施加最小扰动，使预训练流后续演化满足目标约束，同时尽量保持原生成分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受约束生成任务要求样本满足观测或物理规律等条件，但现有方法往往以明显偏离预训练数据分布为代价来强化约束满足。论文旨在缓解约束满足与分布保真之间的权衡。

Method: 将约束执行表述为对预训练流轨迹的最小干预：寻找一个中间状态的最小扰动，使其在不改变预训练流场的情况下继续演化并满足目标约束。通过伴随方法推导扰动的闭式表达，避免昂贵的迭代优化；并自适应选择干预时刻，权衡所需扰动大小及其经剩余流演化后的放大效应。

Result: 摘要称，在生成视觉和物理系统建模等多类任务中，MintFlow取得有竞争力的约束满足效果，并且相比当前先进受约束方法，更好地保持预训练生成分布。摘要未提供具体指标或数值。

Conclusion: MintFlow通过仅调整流轨迹中的中间状态、保持预训练流场不变，实现训练无关的约束采样；其核心优势是减少满足约束所需的额外分布偏移。

Abstract: Flow matching models excel at generative modeling, and many downstream applications require their samples to satisfy prescribed constraints, such as observed measurements and physical laws. However, existing constrained samplers often face a trade-off: \textit{enforcing constraints can substantially displace samples from the pretrained data distribution}. To address this trade-off, we introduce \textbf{MintFlow}, a training-free constrained sampling framework that formulates constraint enforcement as a minimal intervention on the pretrained flow trajectory. MintFlow seeks the minimal perturbation of an intermediate flow state such that its subsequent evolution under the pretrained flow field satisfies the target constraint. By minimally perturbing the flow state while keeping the pretrained flow field unchanged, MintFlow enforces the constraint while minimizing unnecessary deviation from the pretrained distribution. An adjoint formulation yields a closed-form expression for this perturbation, eliminating expensive iterative optimization. Furthermore, MintFlow adaptively selects the intervention time to balance the required perturbation magnitude with its amplification by the remaining flow. Across a range of tasks in generative vision and physical system modeling, MintFlow achieves competitive constraint satisfaction while preserving the pretrained generative distribution substantially better than state-of-the-art constrained methods.

</details>


### [165] [Fast Models, Slow Evidence: A Paired and Self-Audited Evaluation of System-1 Decision Models for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02267)
*Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对两种 System-1 决策模型在 11 类智能体决策任务上的表现进行了配对评估，并复核部署效益分析。Jev 在多数任务上优于 Laya；审计发现若干夸大效益或误读结果的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流包含大量简单、类型明确的判断，若能用单次前向传播的 System-1 模型替代大语言模型调用，可能降低成本和延迟。但这类模型是否准确、稳健，以及其部署收益是否被可靠评估，仍需系统验证。

Method: 在 11 个决策点上构建配对评测，数据来自 18 个公开来源，包含 7,283 个基础样例及 6,640 个鲁棒性变体；对开源权重模型 Laya 和托管模型 Jev 使用字节级一致输入，并进行配对检验、跨硬件与跨日复现检查。另对评测与成本分析流程进行审计。

Result: Jev 在 11 项任务中的 9 项显著更准确，优势为 10.8 至 46.0 个百分点。两者在零样本模型路由上均未超过随机水平，在 RAG 相关性门控上表现持平。Laya 对选项顺序较敏感（顺序反转时 30% 答案改变），面对大量或相似候选时明显退化；在 50 个近邻工具的设置下准确率为 31%，而 Jev 在存在唯一正确工具的样例上为 98%。审计发现：漏算预筛选成本使节省比例从实际 4.3% 被报为 23.9%；将门控准确率误当端到端质量（58% 对 98%）；使用样本内阈值导致留出集漏检最高达 17%（目标为 5%）；所谓注入误报“通道效应”在使用通道原生内容后消失。另两项疑似混杂因素未改变结论。

Conclusion: 结果表明，System-1 模型的表现高度依赖具体决策任务，不能仅凭单项门控指标推断整体部署质量或成本收益。Jev 在多数任务上表现更强，但模型路由、选项顺序敏感性及候选规模仍是明显限制；部署结论应基于端到端评估、严格的留出集阈值验证和完整成本核算。

Abstract: Agent harnesses make many small, typed decisions per task: which model to call, which tool to use, whether retrieved text is relevant, whether an input carries an injection. System-1 decision models answer such questions in a single forward pass with class probabilities, promising large cost and latency savings over LLM calls. We present a paired evaluation of an open-weight (Laya) and a hosted (Jev) System-1 model on 11 agent decision points built from 18 public sources: 7,283 base cases plus 6,640 robustness variants, with byte-identical inputs, paired tests, and cross-hardware and cross-day reproducibility checks. Jev is significantly more accurate on 9 of 11 decision points (+10.8 to +46.0 pp). Neither model beats chance on zero-shot model routing, and they tie on RAG relevance gating. Laya changes 30% of its answers when the option order is reversed and degrades sharply with many or similar candidates (31% at 50 nearest-neighbour tools, vs. 98% for Jev on items with a unique correct tool). We also audit our own pipeline. Three analysis errors and one design confound distorted headline deployment claims: an omitted pre-screen cost (reported 23.9% saving, actual 4.3%), gate accuracy reported as end-to-end quality (58% vs. 98%), in-sample thresholds (5% target, up to 17% held-out misses), and a "channel effect" on injection false positives that vanishes with channel-native content. Two other suspected confounds did not change the conclusions. All cases, raw outputs and analysis code are available at https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval.

</details>


### [166] [The AI Risk Observatory: What Can We Learn from AI Disclosures in Annual Reports About Societal Resilience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02281)
*Bart Jaworski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用两阶段LLM分类流程分析英国上市公司年报，发现2020—2025年AI风险与应用披露均显著增加，但实质性风险披露占比很低，且行业与市场板块差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会韧性研究需要可用、可操作的数据。本文关注能否大规模处理年报，以获得企业如何披露其AI应对情况的有效信号。

Method: 对2020—2025年及部分2026年数据中的1,362家英国上市公司共9,821份年报，应用可复现的两阶段分类流程；以474个人工标注段落验证方法，并进一步分类评估披露的实质性。

Result: 验证显示召回率较高、标签层面一致性中等。2020至2025年，提及AI风险的年报比例由2.8%升至41.2%，披露AI采用的比例由13.8%升至45.2%；供应商提及集中于少数大型厂商，微软居首。披露在关键国家基础设施行业及市场板块间差异显著：AIM市场、能源和数据基础设施领域的AI风险披露较少。全样本仅有7份报告披露AI危害。尽管2025年有41.2%的报告提及AI风险，只有4.3%被判定为实质性披露。

Conclusion: 年报经LLM规模化处理可用于观察企业AI披露趋势，但披露频率不等于披露质量。研究突出显示，AI风险披露虽快速增长，实质性内容仍稀少，且存在明显的行业和市场分层。

Abstract: Societal resilience research relies on access to useful and actionable data, which motivates our main research question: Can annual reports, processed at scale with LLMs, provide a useful signal about how companies disclose their response to AI? We test this by applying a reproducible two-stage classification pipeline to 9,821 annual reports from 1,362 UK listed companies (2020-2025, with partial 2026 data). We first validate the method against 474 human-annotated passages, finding high recall and moderate label-level agreement. We then report three empirical patterns: (i) between 2020 and 2025, the share of reports mentioning AI risk rose from 2.8% to 41.2%, while AI adoption disclosure also rose, from 13.8% to 45.2%, and named vendor mentions cluster around a small set of major providers led by Microsoft; (ii) disclosure varies substantially by Critical National Infrastructure sector and market segment: AIM reports disclose AI risk at far lower rates than Main Market reports, and sectors such as Energy and Data Infrastructure lag behind the rest in AI risk disclosure; and (iii) harm disclosures are near-absent (seven reports across the entire corpus). We develop a substantiveness classification to assess the quality of the disclosure and find that most AI risk disclosure is not substantive: in 2025, 41.2% of all reports mention AI as a risk, but only 4.3% contain AI risk disclosure we classify as substantive.

</details>


### [167] [Keep It CALM: Analyzing the Limits of Global Unsafety in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02300)
*NaHyeon Park,Minhyun Lee,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，统一移除全局不安全信号存在覆盖范围与良性内容保真度之间的权衡，并提出无需训练的 CALM，通过提示词局部反事实修正提升安全性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练文本生成图像安全措施常使用可复用的全局不安全方向或子空间。论文分析发现，狭窄子空间难以覆盖多样的不安全语义，而扩大范围又会干扰与安全边界相近的良性提示词。

Method: 提出 CALM（Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation）。该方法利用匹配的不安全—良性锚点，将提示词路由至相关的不安全类别；仅对发生违规的词元表示进行最小幅度修正，使其朝安全侧移动，并抑制与不安全方向正向对齐的残差分量。

Result: 摘要称，在广泛评估中，CALM 显著提升了不安全内容抑制效果，同时保留了良性提示词的生成效用；未提供具体数据或基线细节。

Conclusion: 相较于对所有提示词统一移除全局不安全信号，基于提示词局部反事实修正的方法更具选择性，有望更好地兼顾安全防护与良性内容质量。

Abstract: Training-free safeguards for text-to-image generation often rely on a reusable safety signal, such as an unsafe direction or global toxic subspace, applied broadly across prompts. We provide a controlled geometric analysis of this global-unsafety assumption and reveal a consistent coverage-selectivity trade-off: compact unsafe subspaces fail to cover heterogeneous unsafe semantics, whereas broader aggregation increasingly distorts safety-adjacent benign prompts. Motivated by this finding, we propose CALM (Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation), a training-free safeguard that replaces uniform global removal with prompt-local counterfactual correction. Using matched unsafe-benign anchors, CALM routes each prompt to active unsafe categories, minimally edits only violating token representations toward the safe side, and suppresses positively aligned unsafe residual components. Across broad evaluation, CALM significantly improves unsafe content suppression while preserving benign utility, demonstrating that local counterfactual correction provides a more selective alternative to global unsafe signal removal.

</details>


### [168] [Choosing Before Acting: Comparative Value Estimation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02330)
*Yu Li,Zheng Zhang,Xin Liu,Shengtian Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CITA，通过比较候选工具调用在当前上下文中的长期价值，改善长程工具使用中的决策与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程工具调用会逐步改变任务状态，最终结果奖励难以将功劳准确分配到各步骤。可靠的步骤级监督通常依赖人工或模型判断，或额外执行多轮轨迹；而现有日志只记录实际采取的调用，缺少同一上下文下对备选调用的比较监督。

Method: 提出 Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents（CITA），训练比较推理模型（CIM）。训练信号结合已观察到的工具行为、贝叶斯工具图模拟器生成的可扩展监督，以及基于大语言模型的语义比较判断。CIM据此估计候选的下一步工具调用在当前上下文中支持最终任务成功的可能性。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准和多个骨干大语言模型上，CITA持续提升 Tool F1 与任务成功率。补充分析显示，CIM能够较准确地估计不同工具调用选择的步骤级价值。

Conclusion: 通过对候选工具调用进行比较式、前瞻性的长期价值估计，CITA弥补了仅凭已执行轨迹训练所缺乏的反事实比较监督，为长程工具使用提供了更有效的决策信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on long-horizon tool invocation sequences for complex tasks, where each invocation can alter the task state and condition subsequent decisions. In long-horizon tool use, final-outcome rewards provide weak credit assignment over long interaction traces. Step-level rewards can offer more targeted feedback, but obtaining reliable step supervision often requires human or LLM judgment, or additional rollouts to estimate the downstream effect of an intermediate decision. In this paper, we argue that effective tool-use agents should estimate the long-horizon value of a possible next tool invocation before executing it. This objective requires comparative supervision over alternative invocations under the same context, while logged trajectories only contain the invocation that was actually taken. Therefore, we propose Comparative Inference for Tool-use Agents (CITA). CITA trains a Comparative Inference Model (CIM) from paired signals that combine observed tool behavior, scalable supervision from a Bayesian tool-graph simulator, and semantic judgments from LLM-based comparison. The resulting CIM learns to estimate how likely a possible next tool invocation is to support final task success under the current context. Across three tool-use benchmarks and multiple backbone LLMs, CITA consistently improves Tool F1 and task success. Additional analysis shows that CIM learns accurate step-level value estimates for comparative tool choices.

</details>


### [169] [World Editing: Intervening on Executable Worlds at Increasing Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02331)
*Max Ku,Nok-Kan Law,Yu-Chien Tang,Shih-Ying Yeh,Ping Nie,Andy Zheng,Tat Hei Lai,Fei-Yueh Chen,Nikko Yu,Wei-Chieh Sun,Suzy Huang,Chiao-Wei Hsu,Chih-Chuan Huang,Chak-Wing Mak,Ho Yin Sam Ng,Edisy Kin Wai Chan,Min-Hung Chen,Ho Kei Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并评测对既有可执行游戏世界进行编辑的能力。基于 Minecraft 和 Terraria 的基准测试显示，强模型能完成多数任务，但编辑越深入，可靠性越低；视觉一致性尤其薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型在生成环境和环境中行动方面已有进展，但针对既有可执行世界进行有目标的修改、同时保留无关属性的研究不足。论文将世界编辑视为区别于世界生成和交互的独立能力。

Method: 提出“干预深度”概念，用以描述编辑对实体、动态机制及系统的耦合程度；构建行业级游戏模组编辑能力及 IGMWorld，并发布 IGMBench，包含 Minecraft 和 Terraria 中的 110 项任务及超过 1,100 条可执行状态与行为评估标准。任务涵盖属性、实体、动态机制和系统干预，并通过确定性可执行性、行为、保留性和视觉检查进行评估。

Result: 最强配置按严格的任务级标准完成率为 78.2%，按单项评估标准计算为 94.8%。干预深度越高，可靠性通常越低；在评估标准数量相近的任务中也观察到这一趋势。多数失败编辑仍可成功构建和加载，表明主要难点在于实现指定行为，而非确保程序可运行。所有受测配置的联合视觉通过率均低于 50%。

Conclusion: 可执行游戏为研究世界编辑提供了实用测试平台。实验显示，世界编辑不能简单等同于世界生成或交互：模型虽已具备较强的编辑能力，但对复杂干预的行为控制和视觉一致性仍存在明显局限。

Abstract: Interactive world models are increasingly capable of generating environments and acting within them, yet deliberately editing an existing executable world remains underexplored. We formulate world editing as intervening on an existing world while preserving properties that should remain unchanged, and introduce intervention depth as an axis describing how strongly an edit couples world entities, dynamics, and systems. We instantiate this capability through industry-grade game modding and introduce IGMWorld, together with IGMBench, a benchmark of 110 tasks and over 1.1K executable state and behavioral criteria across Minecraft and Terraria. The tasks span property, entity, dynamics, and system interventions and are evaluated through deterministic executability, behavioral, preservation, and visual checks. Frontier coding agents already exhibit substantial world-editing capability: the strongest configuration solves 78.2% of tasks under a strict task-level criterion, while criterion-level performance reaches 94.8%. Reliability generally decreases with intervention depth, and this pattern persists even among tasks with similar numbers of evaluation criteria. Most failed edits still build and load successfully, suggesting that the main difficulty is making the edited world behave as requested. Visual consistency remains a separate weakness, with all evaluated configurations below 50% joint visual pass rate. These results show that world editing is a distinct capability from world generation and interaction, and that executable games provide a practical testbed for studying it.

</details>


### [170] [A Multi Method Importance and Performance Efficiency Analysis of Topological Metrics for Natural Visibility Graph Based Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02342)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过四种特征重要性方法的共识排序，从21项自然可视图（NVG）拓扑指标中筛选紧凑子集。CICIDS2018数据集上的CNN与分层五折交叉验证显示，Top3在本实验中的平均分类指标优于Full21，且总运行时间减少96.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVG拓扑指标数量较多，但各指标对网络攻击分类的贡献并不相同；全面提取指标可能增加计算开销。研究旨在判断能否减少指标数量，同时维持或提升分类效果与计算效率。

Method: 评估21项NVG拓扑指标，并结合SHAP、分组置换重要性、Boruta和递归特征消除（RFE），通过共识排序确定重要性。构建Full21、Top15、Top10、Top7、Top5和Top3六种特征配置，在CICIDS2018上使用CNN分类器和分层五折交叉验证进行比较。

Result: 排名前三的指标为avg_clustering_coeff_median、avg_clustering_coeff_std和avg_clustering_coeff_mean。Top3的平均准确率、加权F1和MCC分别为97.148%、97.055%和0.9675，高于Full21的95.999%、95.521%和0.9549。总运行时间由14,961.39秒降至589.22秒，减少96.06%。

Conclusion: 在所测试的数据集、分类器和验证设置下，基于重要性筛选NVG指标可构造更精简且计算成本更低的特征表示；Top3还取得了更高的观测平均分类性能。结果是否适用于其他数据集与模型，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Natural Visibility Graph (NVG) based analysis characterizes network traffic through topological descriptors reflecting different structural properties. However, not all descriptors contribute equally to cyber-attack classification, and extracting a large metric set can increase computational cost. This study evaluates 21 NVG derived topological metrics and investigates whether a compact subset can preserve classification capability while improving computational efficiency. Four importance analysis methods SHAP, grouped Permutation Importance, Boruta, and Recursive Feature Elimination (RFE) are integrated through a Consensus Ranking strategy. Based on this ranking, Full21, Top15, Top10, Top7, Top5, and Top3 configurations are evaluated using the CICIDS2018 dataset, a CNN classifier, and stratified 5 fold cross validation. The three highest ranked metrics are avg_clustering_coeff_median, avg_clustering_coeff_std, and avg_clustering_coeff_mean. Top3 achieved the highest observed mean performance, with 97.148% accuracy, 97.055% weighted F1 score, and an MCC of 0.9675, compared with 95.999%, 95.521%, and 0.9549 for Full21, respectively. It also reduced total runtime from 14,961.39 s to 589.22 s (96.06%). These results indicate that importance guided metric reduction can provide a compact NVG representation with higher observed mean predictive performance and substantially lower computational cost under the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [171] [DeReAct: Decomposed Reasoning and Acting for Reliable AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02351)
*Ajay Vohra,Tao Chen,Neeti Narayan,Caron Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeReAct，将动作审核和任务完成判定从单一 ReAct 策略中拆分为独立门控模块。实验显示，它主要提升较弱模型的 Pass@1；较强模型上准确率大致持平，但轨迹更有依据、约束满足度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ReAct 将动作生成、环境交互和完成判定耦合在一个 LLM 策略中，导致动作授权与结束控制难以独立约束，错误可能逐步传播，且缺乏证据支持的完成声明可能提前终止任务。

Method: 提出模块化架构 DeReAct：Critic 在动作执行前审核拟议动作；Context Manager 重建由环境证据支持的状态（State），并据此认证任务是否完成。

Result: 在 GAIA 和 SWE-bench Verified 上，较弱 Brain 模型的 Pass@1 提升最明显：Qwen3-Coder-480B 提升 6.5–7.0 个百分点，Claude Sonnet 4.5 提升 4.2–5.2 个百分点；随着 Brain 能力增强，收益减小。Claude Opus 4.5 上，Pass@1 与 ReAct 大致相当，但 DeReAct 生成的轨迹证据更完整、对约束的满足度更高。轨迹与消融分析指出，外部门控的效果取决于目标失效模式是否足够常见，以及门控策略本身是否具备足够能力。

Conclusion: DeReAct 通过分离动作审核与完成认证，改善较弱智能体的任务表现，并在较强模型上保留更充分的证据落地能力；其完成控制可能以延后终止为代价，换取更可靠的任务依据。

Abstract: ReAct-based agents typically rely on a single LLM policy to propose actions, interact with the environment, and decide when a task is complete. This coupling makes action authorization and completion control difficult to enforce independently, allowing errors to propagate and unsupported completion claims to terminate execution. We introduce DeReAct, a modular agent architecture that externalizes two gating policies: a Critic that validates proposed actions before execution, and a Context Manager that reconstructs an environment-supported \textsc{State} and certifies task completion.
  Across GAIA and SWE-bench Verified, DeReAct improves Pass@1 most for weaker Brain models, with gains of 6.5--7.0 points for Qwen3-Coder-480B and 4.2--5.2 points for Claude Sonnet~4.5; gains diminish as Brain capability increases. Trajectory and ablation analyses show that external gating is effective when targeted failures are sufficiently prevalent and the gating policy is itself sufficient. With Claude Opus~4.5, Pass@1 remains comparable to ReAct, while DeReAct produces more evidence-complete and constraint-satisfying trajectories, indicating that completion control can trade earlier termination for stronger grounding. Overall, DeReAct improves weaker agents while retaining grounding benefits as models strengthen.

</details>


### [172] [Traversing the Satisfaction-Diversity Frontier in Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02372)
*Kevin Zhai,Siva Rajesh Kasa,Soumya Roy,Sumit Negi,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SatisDive，一种无需训练的推理时方法，在保证每张生成图像达到奖励下限的同时，提升图像批次的多样性；实验显示其在多个设定下优于 FK steering。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本生成图像需要同时满足用户偏好与图像间的视觉多样性。现有方法通常分别优化二者，或将其合并为单一分数，导致高多样性可能掩盖个别候选图像的低奖励。

Method: 将生成问题建模为满足式优化：每张图像均须达到奖励下限，整批图像须达到多样性阈值。奖励下限可调节最差候选奖励与批次多样性的权衡，并定义帕累托前沿。提出无需训练的推理时方法 SatisDive：依据批次相对奖励阈值划分候选，对阈值以下候选侧重提升奖励，对阈值以上候选侧重增加多样性。

Result: 在 Pick-a-Pic 上，在 DreamSim 相近时，SatisDive 相较 FK steering 将最差候选奖励最高提升 0.43（FLUX.1-dev 与 HPSv3）或 0.70（SANA-1.6B 与 ImageReward）。在各设定的 DreamSim 重叠区间内，其满意度—多样性曲线均帕累托支配 FK steering。

Conclusion: 通过显式设置奖励下限并分别处理低奖励与高奖励候选，SatisDive 能更有效地兼顾单张图像质量与整批图像多样性；实验支持其相较基线具有更优的权衡表现。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation enables users to explore several images generated from the same prompt. For these generated images to be useful, each one must reflect the user's preferences, measured by a learned reward, and differ visually from the others to maintain diversity. Existing methods are limited: they either address reward and diversity separately or combine them in one aggregate score, enabling high diversity to offset low rewards. In this paper, we address these limitations by formulating generation as satisficing: every image (candidate) must satisfy a reward floor and the batch of images must satisfy a diversity cutoff. The reward floor controls the balance between worst-candidate reward and batch diversity; we show that varying this floor defines a Pareto frontier. To traverse this frontier, we introduce SatisDive, a training-free inference-time method. SatisDive uses a batch-relative reward cutoff to distinguish lower- from higher-reward candidates, emphasizing reward improvement for candidates below the cutoff and diversity among candidates above it. On Pick-a-Pic, at matched DreamSim, SatisDive improves worst-candidate reward over FK steering by up to 0.43 with FLUX.1-dev as the base model and HPSv3 as the reward, and by up to 0.70 with SANA-1.6B as the base model and ImageReward as the reward. More broadly, across their overlapping DreamSim ranges, SatisDive's satisfaction-diversity curve Pareto-dominates FK steering's curve in each setting.

</details>


### [173] [THPL: A Vision-to-Language Decision Support Framework for Rainbow Trout Feeding Management in RAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02378)
*Meng Liang,Guanbo Feng,Haozhuang Chi,Shilong Zhao,Zhixin Xiong,Yuhang He,Wenfeng Han,Tianhao Zhao,Zhihong Ma,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 THPL 框架，融合虹鳟鱼行为轨迹、环境信息与专家规则，并借助微调后的大语言模型生成可解释的精准投喂决策。实验显示，行为活动系数与专家标注高度相关，双重行为表征及反事实偏好优化均提升决策表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环水养殖系统中的精准投喂有助于降低成本、改善鱼类福利，但既有方法未能充分把鱼类行为与管理知识关联起来，导致决策难以转化为可执行、可解释的投喂方案。

Method: 提出 THPL：首先用 Fishsort 提取鱼群轨迹并计算活动系数（AC）；其次通过层级行为编码器（HBE），使用 Temporal Transformer 和 Set Transformer 表征个体时间变化与群体动态，形成显式物理 token 和隐式软 token；最后将这些 token 与环境参数、元数据及专家规则结合，利用 LoRA 微调大语言模型，并通过反事实多模态直接偏好优化（mDPO）强化因果推理。

Result: AC 与专家标注的投喂强度呈显著正相关（Spearman ρ=0.925，p<0.001）。决策准确率从纯文本基线的 33.33% 提升至使用双重证据 token 时的 93.33%；进一步采用反事实 mDPO 后达到 96.67%。相较标准 LoRA，mDPO 将 METEOR 从 58.10% 提升至 85.30%，Self-BLEU-2 从 58.79% 降至 52.88%，Distinct-3 从 6.68% 升至 7.81%。

Conclusion: 连续时空运动信息为大语言模型提供必要的行为物理依据；结合双重行为表征、专家知识和反事实偏好优化，可提升投喂决策准确性、多样性与因果一致性，并抑制模板化和执行偏差。该框架为精准水产养殖提供了一种决策支持思路。

Abstract: In Recirculating Aquaculture Systems (RAS), precision feeding is critical for minimizing costs and improving fish welfare. However, existing methods lack cognitive alignment between fish behaviors and management knowledge, impeding translation into executable, interpretable feeding decisions. To address this, we propose THPL, a generative feeding decision framework tailored for rainbow trout (Oncorhynchus mykiss) in RAS. First, Fishsort extracts trajectories to establish an Activity Coefficient (AC) quantifying feeding intensity. Second, a Hierarchical Behavior Encoder (HBE) models individual temporal progression and collective dynamics using Temporal and Set Transformers, transforming trajectory tensors into dual-evidence representations of explicit physical and implicit soft tokens. Finally, these tokens are integrated with environmental parameters, metadata, and expert rules to fine-tune an LLM via LoRA, followed by counterfactual multimodal Direct Preference Optimization (mDPO) to reinforce causal reasoning. Results show that AC exhibits a statistically significant monotonic positive correlation with expert-annotated feeding intensity (Spearman $ρ= 0.925$, $p < 0.001$). Ablations indicate that decision accuracy improves from 33.33% (text-only baseline) to 93.33% with dual-evidence tokens, confirming that continuous spatiotemporal tokens provide necessary physical grounding for LLMs. Compared with standard LoRA, counterfactual mDPO elevates decision accuracy from 93.33% to 96.67%, advances METEOR from 58.10% to 85.30%, reduces Self-BLEU-2 from 58.79% to 52.88%, and increases Distinct-3 from 6.68% to 7.81%, suppressing templating and actuation biases while reinforcing causal consistency and operational safety. Overall, by integrating continuous kinematics with LLM reasoning, this study provides a novel decision support paradigm for precision aquaculture.

</details>


### [174] [FlashSinkhorn 2: Block-Sparse Entropic Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02395)
*Felix X. -F. Ye,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Naigang Wang,Davis Wertheimer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FlashSinkhorn 2（FS2），通过粗到细的两阶段求解，在单张 GPU 上高效求解大规模离散熵正则最优传输问题；其在合成测试和宇宙学粒子数据上达到较高规模与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式 GPU 求解器虽避免存储稠密核矩阵，但 Sinkhorn 每轮仍计算全部 n×m 点对，计算成本随问题规模迅速增长。论文希望降低点级迭代开销，同时保持可控的边缘残差精度。

Method: FS2 采用两阶段策略。粗阶段在网格单元质心上求解，将势函数提升到所有点；若抽样边缘检验未通过，则继续在质心上迭代，以减少大部分点级更新。随后，块稀疏精细阶段修正粗阶段无法消除的误差。精细阶段使用 Morton 顺序组织数据块、筛选低贡献块并融合张量核计算；筛选阈值依据块质量设定，从而约束被省略块对每行和每列的贡献。

Result: 合成基准中，FS2 在 32 个问题上均达到目标残差，而 GeomLoss multiscale 在 10 个问题上达到目标。在单张 A100 上，FS2 用不到 2.5 小时求解两个各含 1.34×10^8 个粒子的宇宙学 N 体模拟离散 EOT 问题；熵模糊参数设为平均粒子间距，全粒子边缘残差低于 0.01。

Conclusion: FS2 通过粗粒度初始化与可控误差的块稀疏细化，显著减少大规模 EOT 的计算负担，并展示了单 GPU 在超大粒子规模下实现实用精度的能力。作者称其据所知是在数小时内达到该精度的最大离散 EOT 求解实例，并公开了实现代码。

Abstract: Streaming GPU solvers for entropic optimal transport (EOT), such as FlashSinkhorn, avoid storing the dense kernel but still evaluate all $n\times m$ point pairs in every Sinkhorn iteration. We present \textbf{FlashSinkhorn~2} (FS2), a solver for squared-Euclidean cost on low-dimensional point clouds that solves large discrete EOT problems to a prescribed marginal residual on a single GPU by coupling two stages. A coarse stage solves on cell centroids, lifts the potentials to every point and, when a sampled marginal check rejects the lift, continues on the centroids, replacing most point-level updates. A block-sparse fine stage then removes the centroid error that coarse updates cannot. Its Morton-ordered blocks support screening and fused tensor-core execution, and a threshold set by the block masses bounds each omitted tile's contribution to every row and column. On synthetic benchmarks, FS2 reaches the target residual on all 32 problems and GeomLoss multiscale on 10. On one A100, FS2 solves discrete EOT between two $1.34\times10^8$-particle measures from a cosmological $N$-body simulation, at an entropic blur equal to the mean interparticle distance, to an all-particle marginal residual below 0.01 in under 2.5 hours. To our knowledge, it is the largest discrete EOT problem solved to this accuracy within hours. For reproducibility, we release an open-source implementation at https://github.com/ot-triton-lab/flash-sinkhorn

</details>


### [175] [When Terminal-Agent Training Stalls: Demystifying Data Generation and Verification Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02405)
*Xi Qin,Isabel Kurth,Xin Cui,Elin Park,Alexander Schaefer,Yaad Oren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，终端智能体训练中的可运行环境与测试套件并不等于可靠的端到端流程；任务难度校准、验证器审计和基础设施错误核算都应纳入核心评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前常用前沿模型作为元智能体生成终端任务和验证器，但仅确认 Docker 镜像可运行、测试套件可执行，无法保证训练流水线忠实、稳健且奖励信号合理。论文聚焦由此产生的基准无效、训练框架脆弱和奖励错配三类问题。

Method: 构建并分析一个元智能体流水线，调整提示词并扩展上下文以改善任务可解性；测试 9B 模型在 Claude Opus 生成任务上的表现，并通过加入高难度任务观察任务难度分布变化对通过率的影响。

Result: 提示词重设计和上下文扩展使基线可解性提高 5.6 倍。9B 模型在 20 步内的平均 pass@2 达到 81.3% 后趋于饱和；在不改变训练配置的情况下加入困难任务，平均 pass@2 降至 20.6%，表明可解性区间具有模型特异性。

Conclusion: 元智能体流水线的可靠性评估应将可解性区间校准、验证器审计及基础设施错误核算作为一等指标，而非事后诊断。

Abstract: Using a frontier model like Claude Opus as a meta-agent to generate terminal tasks and verifiers for RL training is increasingly common. Yet a runnable Docker image and executable test suite do not guarantee a faithful end-to-end pipeline for terminal agent training. We present a meta-agent pipeline motivated by this gap, diagnosing three classes of failure: benchmark invalidity, harness brittleness, and reward misalignment. Prompt redesign and context extension raise baseline solvability 5.6 times, but a 9B model saturates at 81.3% mean pass@2 within 20 steps on Claude Opus-generated tasks. Adding hard tasks reduces mean pass@2 to 20.6% without changing the training configuration, a strong evidence that the solvability band is model-specific. These findings demonstrate that meta-agent reliability requires solvability-band calibration, verifier audits, and infrastructure error accounting as first-class evaluation criteria, not post-hoc diagnost.

</details>


### [176] [Reinforcement Learning Techniques for the Optimization of Target Polarization in Nuclear Physics Scattering Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02452)
*Armen Kasparian,Torri Jeske,Monibor Rahman,Chris Keith,James Maxwell,Thomas Britton,Malachi Schram,David Lawrence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将代理模型与强化学习结合，用于动态极化靶微波频率的自动优化；高斯过程能提供不确定性估计并识别分布外区域，强化学习策略据称使优化效果接近操作员的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态极化靶的极化度会因辐射损伤及材料性质变化而改变，因此微波频率需要持续调节。传统依赖专家人工试错，耗时且难以扩展到不同靶样品；需要一种数据驱动、能够处理预测不确定性的自动控制方法。

Method: 使用APOLLO低温靶系统运行数据，训练多层感知机（MLP）和高斯过程回归（GPR），以微波频率、束流电流和累积辐射剂量预测极化度。比较模型的预测及分布外识别能力，并构造适用于多个靶样品的高斯过程近似，将代理模型嵌入标准化仿真环境。随后训练强化学习智能体，以基于下置信界的奖励在提升极化表现与控制不确定性之间权衡。

Result: GPR模型给出经过校准的不确定性估计，并能可靠识别训练分布之外的区域；MLP对分布偏移的敏感性有限。作者报告，强化学习智能体的表现相较操作员操作提升近两倍。摘要未说明该倍数的具体指标、对照设计或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 研究展示了以不确定性感知代理模型支撑强化学习控制动态极化靶的可行性，可能减少人工调频试错并改善控制效果。其结论仍需结合摘要未提供的评估指标、验证方案及跨样品泛化结果加以判断。

Abstract: The operation of dynamically polarized targets in nuclear physics experiments relies on continuous tuning of the microwave frequency to compensate for radiation damage and evolving material properties, a task that is traditionally performed through manual trial-and-error by expert operators. This work presents a data-driven control framework that combines surrogate modeling with reinforcement learning to optimize the target polarization. Using operational data from the APOLLO cryogenic target system, we train and evaluate multilayer perceptron and Gaussian process regression models to predict polarization as a function of microwave frequency, beam current, and accumulated radiation dose. We show that Gaussian process-based models provide calibrated uncertainty estimates and reliably identify regions outside the training distribution, while MLPs exhibit limited sensitivity to distributional shift. To enable learning and control across multiple target samples, we introduce a Gaussian process approximation and embed the surrogate model within a standardized simulation environment. A reinforcement learning agent is trained using a lower-confidence-bound reward formulation that balances performance maximization against uncertainty. We are able to show an almost 2x improvement on the operators actions utilizing our RL agent.

</details>


### [177] [Tropical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02478)
*Arip Asadulaev,Aladin Djuhera,Karim Salta,Holger Boche,Fakhri Karray,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出热带强化学习（TROPIC），以最大化替代传统强化学习中奖励轨迹概率求和，使模型能够保留并组合已验证的成功路径；在四项智能体任务上较强的在线策略基线最高提升16个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统期望回报将所有成功轨迹的概率相加，只反映整体成功频率，难以识别具体有效解。概率归一化还可能导致强化一个方案时削弱另一个尚未证伪的方案。对组合推理而言，关键步骤可能分别出现在不同失败尝试中，却很少在单次轨迹里完整出现，因此传统目标不利于跨轨迹组合。

Method: 提出热带强化学习，将备选解的概率求和改为取最大值，对应热带半环。状态价值定义为最可能的、经过验证的解的对数概率，并保留可回放、可复用的显式路径。若最佳前缀与最佳后缀在同一状态衔接，即使来自不同 rollout，也可拼接。为具有确定性、可重置且结果可验证的环境设计训练算法 TROPIC。

Result: 在 Sokoban、Countdown、FrozenLake 和 WebShop 四项智能体任务中，TROPIC 相比最强在线策略基线最高提升16个百分点。摘要未提供各任务的具体数值、统计显著性或消融结果。

Conclusion: 改变强化学习目标所依赖的代数结构，而不只是改进估计方法，有望增强语言模型的组合推理能力。该方法当前适用范围依赖环境具有确定性、可重置性和可验证结果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language models typically maximizes expected return, adding up the probabilities of all successful trajectories. However, the classical sum formulation can only report how often the model policy succeeds, not which solution actually worked, and because probabilities sum to one, reinforcing one solution can make the model forget another that was never shown to be wrong. This makes expected return a poor fit for compositional reasoning, where a solution must be assembled from reasoning steps that the model produces in separate, often failed, attempts but rarely produces together. To address this, we propose Tropical Reinforcement Learning, which rests on a simple change of algebra: instead of adding the probabilities of alternative solutions, we take their maximum, which yields the tropical semiring. The value of a state then becomes the log-probability of its most likely verified solution, together with an explicit path that can be replayed and reused. This enables true composition, since the best prefix and the best suffix meeting at a shared state can be joined even when they come from different rollouts. To put this into practice, we introduce TROPIC, a training algorithm for deterministic, resettable environments with verifiable outcomes. On four agentic tasks (Sokoban, Countdown, FrozenLake, WebShop), TROPIC outperforms the strongest on-policy baselines by up to 16 percentage points. Changing the algebra of reinforcement learning, not just its estimators, can thus substantially improve compositional reasoning in language models

</details>


### [178] [MEA: A Reward-Driven Multi-Agent System for Faithful Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02480)
*Yuyang Cheng,Raghav Kaushik Ravi,Srivarshinee Sridhar,Sriparna Saha,Akash Ghosh,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MEA 多智能体框架，通过选择解释工具并生成忠实于模型行为的自然语言说明，提升跨模态机器学习可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域广泛使用机器学习，但模型预测过程不透明。现有事后解释工具的输出维度高、工具选择复杂，且需要专家综合证据，普通领域专家难以有效使用；同时，前沿大语言模型生成的解释也可能不忠实于模型行为。

Method: MEA 包含 Proposer 与 Actor 两类智能体：Proposer 根据问题类型和数据模态选择、配置解释工具；Actor 经过端到端优化，将工具输出转化为自然语言解释，并以忠实性为优化目标。研究覆盖表格、文本和图像模态，以及特征归因、反事实推理和伪相关特征检测等问题类型；各类任务配有基于扰动的忠实性指标，并采用模态自适应惩罚增强奖励优化。

Result: 在六个数据集上，MEA 整体优于事后解释器、智能体方法及闭源基线。相较未训练的基础模型，奖励驱动优化使忠实性分别提升：表格数据 28%、文本数据 21%、图像数据 34%。研究还发现，前沿大语言模型生成的解释存在系统性不忠实问题。

Conclusion: 智能体可作为机器学习可解释性的可扩展、可适配自然语言接口，帮助降低使用解释工具的门槛，并推动可解释性超越固定用途的单一工具。摘要的核心贡献是跨模态多智能体框架及其忠实性优化；其效果仍需结合具体数据集、评测指标和实际部署情境进一步判断。

Abstract: Recent years have seen the employment of a plethora of machine learning (ML) models in high-stakes domains, but they remain largely opaque to the practitioners who act on their predictions. While post-hoc explanation methods offer a lens into this model behavior, wielding them effectively demands expertise most domain experts lack: navigating high-dimensional outputs, selecting the best explanations, and synthesizing evidence across disparate tools. To this end, we present MEA, a multi-agent framework that removes the explanation knowledge barrier entirely: a Proposer agent selects and configures explanation tools based on the question and modality, while an Actor agent is optimized end-to-end against faithfulness, transforming the outputs into natural language explanations grounded in model behavior across tabular, text, and vision modalities. Further, we introduce diverse question types spanning feature attribution, counterfactual reasoning, and spurious feature detection, each paired with a perturbation-based faithfulness metric. We find that frontier LLMs systematically produce unfaithful explanations. By optimizing against faithfulness rewards augmented with a modality-adaptive penalty, MEA consistently outperforms post hoc explainers, agentic, and closed-source baselines across six datasets, with reward-driven optimization yielding faithfulness gains of +28% (tabular), +21% (text), and +34% (vision) over the untrained backbone. More broadly, our findings suggest that AI agents themselves can serve as a scalable, adaptable interface to ML explainability, opening a path toward natural-language explainability that generalizes beyond the fixed, single-purpose tools that have long defined the field.

</details>


### [179] [What Does a Token Cost? A Mixture-of-Agents Measurement of Sufficient Per-Token Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02491)
*Zhixu Du,Weijia Han,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文衡量生成序列中每个 token 所需的最低充分计算量，并利用该估计优化模型路由与草稿生成，在准确率相近或略优时降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型对每个生成 token 分配相同计算量，但 token 的生成难度不同。投机解码和模型路由等方法假设部分计算可以省去，却缺乏对单个 token 实际所需计算量的测量。

Method: 构建由三个模型家族、十五个不同容量语言模型组成的 Mixture-of-Agents 面板。每个模型在给定正确前序 token 的条件下，逐 token 尝试复现参考序列；将成功复现该 token 的最小模型所对应的推理成本定义为其“充分计算量”，作为实际需求的上界。再将此估计形成的计算需求图用于模型路由和草稿生成。

Result: 三个核心基准中，0.5B 模型可复现参考序列的 92%–95% token。跨 Qwen、OLMo 和 R1 蒸馏模型面板，估计 FLOPs 中最昂贵的 10% token 占 64%–80%。在全部 500 道 MATH-500 题上，基于该计算图的路由将预计延迟从 7.59 秒降至 5.12 秒，且准确率略有提升，优于最佳置信度路由基线。用于草稿生成时，草稿 token 数减少 32.6%，预计延迟降低约 20%，准确率相近。

Conclusion: token 所需计算量存在显著差异，当前均匀分配计算的方式留有优化空间。基于充分计算量结构进行计算分配，有望改善推理效率；论文结果也为进一步开发自适应控制器提供了依据。

Abstract: Large language models spend the same amount of computation on every token they generate, regardless of how difficult each token is to produce. Methods such as speculative decoding and model routing are built on the premise that much of this computation is unnecessary, yet the computation an individual token actually requires has not been measured. We measure it through a Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) lens: a panel of fifteen language models of increasing capacity, drawn from three families, in which every agent attempts to reproduce a reference sequence token by token, conditioned on the correct preceding tokens. We define the inference cost of the smallest agent that succeeds as the token's sufficient compute, which upper-bounds what the token requires. On three core benchmarks, a 0.5B agent reproduces 92--95\% of reference tokens. Across Qwen, OLMo, and R1-distilled panels, the most expensive 10\% account for 64--80\% of estimated FLOPs. On all 500 MATH-500 problems, the MoA-derived map helps model routing reduce projected latency from 7.59 to 5.12 seconds while slightly improving accuracy, relative to the best confidence-routing baseline. The MoA-map helps drafting use 32.6\% fewer draft tokens and approximately 20\% lower projected latency than fixed-window drafting at similar accuracy. These comparisons reveal remaining allocation headroom, motivating controllers that exploit sufficient-compute structure.

</details>


### [180] [Right Order, Wrong Scale: Auditing LLM Judges for Occupational AI Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02492)
*Harry Lyu,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 O*NET-BENCH 审计 LLM 评审器：排序准确并不代表其接受率或职业层面统计量与工人判断一致；评审器必须针对实际测量目标进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 评审器正被用于判断 AI 输出是否符合工作要求，但通常的响应排序一致性无法证明它能准确估计可接受回答比例，或汇总到任务、职业层面后仍与工人评价相符。

Method: 基于一项包含 45,796 条工人评分的既有调查构建 O*NET-BENCH，在 4,501 条测试评分上评估六个模型家族中的 33 种既有评审配置。分析排序准确率、可接受率估计、任务与职业层面的聚合一致性、交叉验证校准效果及预测辅助估计的精度收益，并在预先设定标准的任务和工人均不重叠验证集上复现关键结果。

Result: 25 种配置的考虑平局排序准确率至少为 0.60，但一个基于训练数据拟合的、仅使用回答文本的 TF-IDF 基线几乎达到最强评审器的水平。评审器估计的可接受率跨度为 3.0%—97.9%，而匹配职业工人的比例为 61.1%。某一微调模型谱系中，从逐条评分改为捆绑式少样本/列表排序协议虽改善排序，却降低了任务和职业层面均值与工人评价的一致性，该反转在独立验证集上复现。交叉验证校准大体消除了均值偏差，但校准分数对个体工人评分方差的解释比例最高仅为 8.5%；在所研究的标注预算下，预测辅助估计带来的精度提升至多较小。

Conclusion: 排序表现不能单独作为职业测量有效性的依据。若要用评审器估计可接受率或职业层面的汇总指标，应直接针对这些目标与工人数据进行验证；校准可纠正总体均值偏差，但不能据此认为模型能够解释个体判断差异。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly used to assess whether AI outputs meet workplace requirements, but agreement on response rankings does not establish agreement on acceptance rates or occupational aggregates. We introduce O*NET-BENCH, an audit suite derived from an existing survey of 45,796 worker ratings, and evaluate 33 pre-existing judge configurations across six model families on 4,501 test ratings. Twenty-five configurations achieve tie-aware pair accuracy of at least 0.60, although a train-fitted response-only TF-IDF baseline nearly matches the strongest judge. Despite this ordering agreement, judges estimate that 3.0%-97.9% of responses are acceptable, compared with 61.1% for occupation-matched workers. In one fine-tuned lineage, changing from pointwise scoring to a bundled few-shot/listwise protocol improves response ordering while reducing agreement with worker means at the task and occupation levels; this reversal replicates on a task- and worker-disjoint validation split under prespecified criteria. Cross-validated calibration largely removes mean bias, but calibrated scores explain at most 8.5% of individual worker-rating variance. Prediction-assisted estimation yields at most small precision gains at the studied label budgets. These results show that ranking agreement alone is insufficient for occupational measurement. Judges should be validated against the acceptance rates and aggregates their scores will be used to estimate.

</details>


### [181] ["I just assumed that it would translate": examining MT risk awareness among healthcare staff with abbreviations as a use case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02496)
*Eleanor Taylor-Stilgoe,Félix do Carmo,Constantin Orăsan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文考察英国医疗工作人员在较高风险医疗场景中使用机器翻译（尤其是 Google 翻译）处理医学缩写的潜在风险。研究通过访谈 21 名不同岗位和专科的医疗工作人员，呈现法语、西班牙语临床语料及其机器翻译结果，并进行定性分析。摘要尚未报告具体研究发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国医疗工作人员会使用机器翻译跨越医患语言障碍，但在医疗记录等高风险场景中，工作人员对其风险的认知研究有限。医学缩写即使在单语交流中也可能引发严重误读，因此可用作识别机器翻译风险的案例。

Method: 研究者从权威临床语料中选取有语境的法语和西班牙语医学缩写实例，使用 Google 翻译处理后，在半结构式访谈中向 21 名来自不同岗位和专科的医疗工作人员展示；随后进行定性分析与交叉分析。

Result: 摘要只说明研究采用了上述访谈和分析方法，没有提供参与者的具体观点、识别出的风险类别或其他实证结果，因此无法据此概括研究发现。

Conclusion: 摘要指出，在医疗记录等高风险场景中，不加审慎地依赖机器翻译可能影响患者安全；医学缩写是探查这一风险的切入点。由于未提供结果和讨论，尚不能判断研究最终得出的结论或提出的建议。

Abstract: In the UK, public healthcare staff report turning to machine translation (MT) - predominantly Google Translate (GT) - to communicate with patients across language barriers. Though intended to support their duty of care, potentially uninformed reliance on MT in such contexts could have serious consequences for patient safety. Research nonetheless remains limited on staff awareness of the possible risks posed by higher-stakes MT use in general and with patient medical records in particular, most existing literature instead examining its use in interpersonal situations or with patient-oriented documentation. Moreover, medical abbreviations are well-documented as increasing patient risk even monolingually, with outcomes from their misuse and/or misinterpretation ranging from temporary harm to the death of the patient. Abbreviations were therefore selected as a use case for identifying the potential risks posed by their translation with MT. Contextualised French and Spanish data examples drawn from authoritative clinical corpora and translated via GT were presented during semi-structured interviews to 21 healthcare staff participants in diverse roles and specialties. The results were then subject to qualitative analysis and cross-analysis.

</details>


### [182] [HXAI: Hierarchical Privacy-Preserving Explainable AI in Distributed Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02504)
*Poushali Sengupta,Sabita Maharjan,Frank Eliassen,Yan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HXAI，一种面向电网需求管理的分层隐私保护可解释框架：细粒度解释留在家庭本地，区域层面聚合解释并通过隐私预算限制重复查询造成的累积泄露。实验表明，该方法能保留区域负荷管理所需信息，同时避免向运营方传输家电级用电数据；关键在于保留解释的语义结构，而非单纯降低数值误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源出力间歇且用电需求具有随机性，电网运营方需要细粒度、可用于决策的家庭用电洞察，以管理峰值负荷和制定响应式电价。然而，细粒度解释可能暴露家庭隐私；常见可解释人工智能方法存在信息泄露风险，而标准隐私保护方法又可能削弱解释的实用性。

Method: 提出分层框架 HXAI，包含两个部分：一是在安全、私密环境中生成细粒度解释的本地模型；二是聚合本地解释、支持电网区域级分析的区域模型。框架通过灵活管理差分隐私预算，限制运营方重复查询导致的累积隐私暴露，并使家电级用电信息留在本地。

Result: 在模拟和真实能源数据集上的实验显示，HXAI 能提供对区域负荷管理有用的洞察，同时不向电网运营方传输家电级消费数据。研究还指出，在差分隐私条件下，保留解释的语义结构比最小化数值误差更重要。

Conclusion: HXAI 为能源管理中的隐私保护与可解释性兼顾提供了一种分层方案：在家庭侧保留细粒度信息，在区域侧提供受隐私约束的决策支持。

Abstract: Balancing electricity demand and supply is increasingly difficult due to the inherent intermittency of renewable power generation and the stochastic power consumption. Grid operators require fine-grained, decision-relevant insights into household energy consumption to manage peak loads and design responsive tariffs, but increased transparency at this level raises significant privacy concerns. Traditional methods for explainable AI (XAI) can reveal sensitive information, while standard privacy techniques often reduce the usefulness of explanations. To address this issue, we introduce HXAI, a hierarchical framework that preserves privacy while enabling reasonable explainable analysis for grid-level demand management. HXAI consists of two main components: (1) a local model that generates fine-grained explanations within a secure, private environment, and (2) a zonal model that aggregates these explanations to support grid-level analysis while enforcing privacy through flexible privacy-budget management. We explicitly limit cumulative privacy exposure under repeated operator queries and show that the proposed framework preserves decision-relevant information without compromising household privacy. Experiments on both simulated and real-world energy datasets demonstrate that HXAI provides useful insights for zonal load management while ensuring that appliance-level consumption remains local and is never transmitted to grid operators. Our results show that preserving the semantic structure of explanations, rather than minimizing numerical error, is the key to XAI under differential privacy. This framework provides a way to achieve both privacy and explainability in energy management.

</details>


### [183] [World Action Modeling with Progressive Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02508)
*Fei Zhang,Zhaochong An,Duncan Frost,Yikai Wang,Pengfei Liu,Ya Zhang,Michal Drozdzal,Amir Bar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProWAM通过联合预测动作与按执行顺序排列的稀疏视觉子目标，为机器人提供持续的视觉进度指引；在多个仿真基准及新场景零样本实机测试中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在长时程任务中常需生成密集视频，计算开销较高；仅预测单张未来图像虽更高效，却缺乏从当前状态逐步推进到目标的过程性指引。

Method: ProWAM联合生成动作与有序稀疏视觉子目标，以子目标锚定任务执行中的动作生成。子目标预测可利用大规模无动作视频训练，使视频骨干承担复杂视觉规划；执行时仅进行一次视频骨干前向计算并缓存子目标特征，重规划阶段只需轻量级动作去噪。

Result: 在仿真中，ProWAM在LIBERO-Plus取得85.8%，在随机化RoboTwin取得75.7%，相对最强基线最高提升35.9%；在RoboCasa365取得48.1%，在Composite-Unseen取得18.2%，总排名第4。新场景零样本实机成功率为70.0%，较最强基线的55.0%提高15个百分点（相对提升27.3%）。

Conclusion: 结果支持以按进度索引的视觉预见引导闭环控制：它兼顾推理效率与长时程任务的过程性指引，并提升分布外鲁棒性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic control by jointly predicting future visual dynamics and actions from an initial observation and instruction. However, existing WAMs struggle with long-horizon prediction, as generating dense video rollouts is highly inefficient. Some recent WAMs address this by predicting a single future frame without generating the full video, but this approach neglects how to progress toward the goal. We present ProWAM, a progressive world action model that jointly predicts actions and an ordered sequence of sparse visual sub-goals, providing explicit visual guidance to anchor action generation throughout task execution. This design scales naturally, as sub-goal prediction can be learned from large-scale action-free videos, allowing the video backbone to offload complex visual planning from the action policy. For efficient action generation, ProWAM executes a single video-backbone forward pass to cache sparse sub-goal features, eliminating iterative full-video generation and requiring only lightweight action denoising during replanning. Across extensive evaluations, ProWAM achieves superior out-of-distribution robustness. On simulation benchmarks, it sets new state-of-the-art results on LIBERO-Plus (85.8%) and randomized RoboTwin (75.7%), outperforming the strongest baseline with relative gains of up to +35.9%. On RoboCasa365, ProWAM achieves a 48.1% success rate and 18.2% on the challenging Composite-Unseen split, ranking 4th overall. Crucially, in zero-shot real-world experiments, ProWAM achieves 70.0% success, outperforming the strongest baseline by +15.0 (from 55.0% to 70.0%, a +27.3% relative gain) in novel scenes. These results demonstrate the value of progress-indexed visual foresight for closed-loop control. Our program is in https://sii-ferenas.github.io/ProWAM-page.

</details>


### [184] [On-Premises Multi-Course RAG Tutoring for Business Education: Hardware-Software Trade-offs in a Campus AI Tutor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02510)
*Sidney Shapiro,Joshua Lindemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍 CourseChat：部署在校园本地基础设施上的多课程 RAG 助教系统，并通过模型、证据忠实度、对话及测验审计进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园 AI 助教需要依据指定课程材料回答，同时确保教材及学生对话留存在校方基础设施内；实践中还需平衡回答质量、响应速度、课程隔离和产品可用性。

Method: 在双边缘 AI 主机上部署 FastAPI、本地向量数据库及 Ollama 托管的本地 LLM，通过校园 Web 网关提供服务，并计划嵌入 Moodle。六个课程班次按各自 CRN 隔离。研究进行了两轮生成模型比较、固定证据条件下的来源忠实度比较，以及对话和测验审计；另以预先编制的题库支持练习。

Result: 部分较大模型未通过课堂响应速度门槛，12B 模型和一个 7B 替代模型通过。混合专家模型虽改善部分纠错表现，却带来新的事实与对话连续性错误，因此研究暂时保留 8B 生产模型，但不声称其普遍最优。软件改进提升了后续话题识别，同时保持课程范围；系统提供覆盖 65 个模块的 435 道预制题。

Conclusion: 模型选择、证据检索、服务兼容性和产品设计应作为整体工程问题共同权衡。现有结果尚不能证明学习成效；教师评价、峰值负载能力及公共网关的完整验收仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Campus AI tutors based on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must ground answers in assigned course materials while keeping textbooks and student dialogue on institutional infrastructure. We present CourseChat, an on-premises, multi-course RAG tutor for undergraduate business education, deployed behind a campus web gateway and intended for use embedded in Moodle. Six isolated course offerings, each keyed by its own course reference number (CRN), share twin-edge AI hosts running a FastAPI service, a local vector database, and a local large language model (LLM) served by Ollama. We report two generation-model bake-off rounds, a separate fixed-evidence source-fidelity comparison, and conversation and quiz audits. Several larger models failed the classroom speed gate, but a 12B model and a 7B alternative passed. A separate mixture-of-experts candidate improved some corrections while introducing new factual and continuity errors. We therefore retain the 8B production model pending a demonstrated overall improvement, rather than claiming that 8B is universally optimal. Software changes improved follow-up topic resolution while preserving course scope; 435 prebuilt questions across 65 modules decouple practice from live generation. The results support treating model choice, evidence selection, serving compatibility, and product design as a joint engineering decision. They do not establish learning gains: faculty ratings, peak-load capacity, and complete public-gateway acceptance remain separate evaluation needs.

</details>


### [185] [Hypothesis-guided discovery of cognitive algorithms via program refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02523)
*Huiwen Alex Yang,Mark K. Ho,Bill D. Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种人机协作的认知算法发现流程：由大语言模型代理在研究者约束下迭代修改概率程序，并通过概率推断检验；在问题解决行为数据上，修订模型的拟合优于初始模型，并发现少量反复出现、能解释行为差异的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知建模方法可解释且能利用人类专家知识，但灵活性和可扩展性不足；纯粹依赖大语言模型从头生成模型虽灵活、可扩展，却缺少专家参与，且此前主要用于比算法恢复更简单的任务。因此，需要兼顾专家指导、模型可解释性与自动化迭代能力的方法。

Method: 将认知算法发现表述为程序精炼问题。研究者提供以概率程序表示的初始认知模型及修改约束；多个大语言模型代理负责识别模型与行为数据之间的不匹配、提出代码层面的修改，并验证修改是否保持结构忠实度。每轮修改后，概率推断模块对潜变量进行推断并计算数据似然，以评估修订模型。

Result: 在一种可展现多种认知算法的问题解决任务中，修订后的模型持续比其初始模型具有更好的拟合度；分析还识别出少量反复出现的模型改进，这些改进能够捕捉有意义的行为差异。

Conclusion: 该混合流程将人类专家约束、LLM代理的代码修改能力与概率推断结合，为从行为数据中发现和精炼认知算法提供了可扩展方案。摘要报告了模型拟合改善及重复出现的有效创新，但未提供具体效应量、基线比较细节或跨任务泛化证据。

Abstract: Developing cognitive models of algorithmic reasoning from behavioral data is a central problem in cognitive science that challenges current methods. Traditional approaches to cognitive modeling are interpretable and benefit from human expertise, but lack flexibility and scalability. Emerging techniques using large language models (LLMs) for de novo generation of cognitive models are scalable and flexible, but lack a role for human expertise and have mostly been applied to simpler tasks than algorithm recovery. We propose a hybrid system that treats discovery of cognitive algorithms as a program refinement problem. Human-created cognitive models are expressed as probabilistic programs and provided to a system of LLM agents with a mandate to: identify mismatches between model and behavior; propose code-level modifications within researcher-specified constraints; and verify structural fidelity. Revisions propagate to a probabilistic inference module that performs inference for latent variables and data likelihood computations. We evaluate the pipeline on human behavior in a problem-solving paradigm that exposes a variety of cognitive algorithms. Revised models consistently improve model fit relative to ancestral models and reveal a small set of recurring innovations that capture meaningful behavioral variability in this task.

</details>


### [186] [Learning What to Investigate Next: Meta-Reasoning for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02525)
*Ankur Samanta,Yonathan Efroni,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MIRA，将长程研究拆分为“元推理决策”与“具体执行”，并进一步通过生成式价值预测和 actor-critic 训练学习下一步研究方向。实验显示其改善长程推理、计算分配与跨环境迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究代理既要执行调查，也要决定调查什么；关键决策在长执行轨迹中出现稀疏，其影响可能延后数轮才显现，因此难以有效学习和分配信用。

Method: MIRA采用分层架构：外层元推理器基于持久研究记录整理上下文，生成下一项调查的工作指令或结束任务；每项指令由新建的内层执行器完成。作者在决策边界处训练生成式 critic，预测部分状态下的预期剩余回报，并利用跨环境预训练形成可迁移的价值先验。随后提出 MIRA-AC，将剩余回报预测与下一步调查选择结合为生成式 actor-critic，在元推理决策层进行强化学习优化。

Result: 不进行策略训练时，MIRA在定理证明和开放式神经架构研究中改善长程推理，并更有效地分配额外计算。边界级生成式 critic 优于 token 级替代方案；跨环境预训练提升价值预测与适应能力。MIRA-AC在四个 autoresearch 环境中利用模型自身的代理式 hill-climbing 信号训练后，提高了真实目标（gold）表现；跨环境价值初始化也支持 actor 迁移。

Conclusion: 将元推理显式建模为研究方向分配策略，为学习长程自主研究提供了有效途径。其核心优势是把学习重点放在少量高层决策上，而不必直接优化这些决策所触发的长执行轨迹。

Abstract: Long-horizon research agents must decide both how to investigate and what to investigate next as evidence accumulates. This is hard to learn because such decisions are sparse in long execution traces, and their consequences may emerge several investigations later. We introduce Meta-reasoning for Iterative Research Agents (MIRA), a hierarchical architecture separating research allocation from execution. An outer-loop meta-reasoner curates context from a persistent research record, then writes a work order for the next investigation or ends the episode. A fresh inner-loop executor carries out each work order, making execution part of the transition between meta-reasoning actions. Without policy training, MIRA improves long-horizon inference and allocates additional compute more effectively in theorem proving and open-ended neural-architecture research. Its decision boundaries also provide natural units for credit assignment. At each boundary, we train a generative critic to forecast expected remaining return from partial states, outperforming token-level alternatives. Cross-environment pretraining improves forecasting and adaptation, yielding a transferable prior for valuing partial progress. We use this prior to initialize MIRA-AC, a generative actor-critic jointly trained to forecast remaining return and choose the next investigation, without a separate critic model. MIRA-AC concentrates policy optimization on meta-reasoning decisions, enabling efficient long-horizon reinforcement learning without directly optimizing the longer execution traces they initiate. Training MIRA-AC on the model's own proxy hill-climbing signals improves gold performance across four autoresearch environments; the actor transfers with cross-environment value initialization. Together, these results show that meta-reasoning can be learned as an explicit policy for directing long-horizon autonomous research.

</details>


### [187] [How To Train Your World Model: Fine-tuning vs RAG for LM-based World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02542)
*Dhananjay Ashok,Shantanu Agarwal,Vivek Datla,Jonathan May,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统比较基于微调与检索增强生成（RAG）的语言模型世界模型，并提出层级检索和混合建模方案；混合方案在多种环境与模型中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本环境中的世界模型可帮助智能体预测行动后果并进行规划。虽然微调语言模型已成为主流做法，RAG等非参数方法广泛应用，但其在语言模型世界建模中的效果、数据效率及局限尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在涵盖具身交互、网页导航和社交场景的五类环境中，系统评估微调与RAG世界模型。考察探索数据数量与多样性的影响；通过反事实干预估计检索阶段错误率；比较不同查询改写策略，并构建结合参数化环境动力学与主动维护记忆检索的混合世界模型。

Result: 微调模型在20种设置中的15种帮助智能体获得更高奖励。两类方法均受益于更多且更多样的探索数据，但RAG数据效率更高，微调则更能从经验数据规模扩大中获益。检索器经常从经验库中取回次优转移；层级查询改写优于传统检索流程。混合系统在多个环境和模型上持续优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 微调与RAG各有优势：前者更善于利用规模化经验，后者数据效率更高但受到检索质量限制。通过层级检索改进查询，并将参数化动力学与动态记忆检索结合，可获得更稳健、整体性能更强的世界模型。

Abstract: World models (WMs) simulate the transition dynamics of environments, enabling agents to plan over the consequences of their actions. In text-based environments, fine-tuning a Language Model (LM) to serve as a WM has emerged as a dominant paradigm. However, despite the widespread success of non-parametric approaches such as Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval for LM-based world modelling remains underexplored. We conduct a systematic evaluation across five diverse environments spanning embodied, web navigation and social settings, comparing fine-tuning and RAG-based approaches for LM-based world modelling. Our study reveals that fine-tuning often outperforms RAG, with fine-tuned WMs enabling agents to obtain higher rewards on 15/20 settings. While both construction paradigms benefit from additional and more diverse exploration, RAG-based approaches prove more data-efficient, and fine-tuning approaches disproportionately benefit from scaling the amount of experience collected. With a focus on RAG-based WMs, we devise a procedure that uses counterfactual intervention to estimate the error rate of the retrieval stage, and show that retrievers consistently surface suboptimal transitions from the experience buffer. Hoping to address this failing, we study a variety of query reformulation strategies, demonstrating that a hierarchical approach outperforms the traditional retrieval pipeline. Finally, we compose our findings into a hybrid world modelling system that parametrically captures core environment dynamics, while learning to rely on retrieval from an actively maintained memory store. Our hybrid system consistently outperforms other methods across multiple environments and models, showcasing the robustness of the approach and the applicability of our findings.

</details>


### [188] [How to Have a Sensitive Debate: An Instance-Optimal Protocol for AI Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02557)
*Jiawei Li,Zhiyang Xun,Lijie Chen,Jonah Brown-Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于 AI 辩论监督的新协议，适用于具有稳定子问题分解的问题；该协议提供最坏情况正确性保证，使诚实且正确成为双方辩手的占优策略，并在仅能黑盒查询人类判断的协议中达到逐实例最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着强大 AI 能力接近或超过人类专家，如何可靠监督 AI 输出日益重要。AI 辩论通过让两个 AI 将复杂问题拆解为便于判断的主张来辅助监督；既有理论协议仍有保证形式和策略性质方面的局限。

Method: 针对可稳定分解为子问题的问题设计新辩论协议；将稳定问题分解与查询复杂性中的分数块敏感度联系起来，并据此证明协议的正确性和黑盒查询下界。

Result: 新协议具有最坏情况而非平均情况的正确性保证；对双方辩手而言，诚实且正确均为占优策略，而非仅构成 Stackelberg 均衡。此外，黑盒下界证明其逐实例最优：在仅黑盒查询人类判断的限制下，同类协议无法表现得更好。

Conclusion: 该工作改进了适用于稳定可分解问题的 AI 辩论理论，并通过分数块敏感度建立协议设计与查询复杂性之间的联系，为其最优性提供了下界依据。

Abstract: As powerful AI systems reach and sometimes surpass the abilities of human experts across a range of cognitively demanding tasks, the problem of accurate oversight and supervision of these systems has become increasingly urgent. One promising approach is AI debate, which seeks to leverage a debate between two powerful AIs to break complex questions down into simpler claims that can be easily judged directly. Theoretical work on debate has formalized this intuition in the language of computational complexity theory, where the goal is to design protocols (i.e., rules of the debate game) that provide rigorous guarantees on correctness for judging solutions to complex problems with limited supervision.
